# CVE catalog

This is the auto-generated reference for every CVE the tarpit-honeypot
catalog knows about — the vulnerabilities it emulates (fake-vulnerable banners
and patch-level advisories) and detects (exploit-attempt signatures matched by
`internal/cve.MatchPayload`). A **detectable** CVE carries an exploit signature
so a live attempt is flagged; the rest are advisory-only (binary/off-path/
credential issues) surfaced for banner and patch-level emulation.

> Generated by `tools/cvedoc` from `internal/cve`. Do not edit by hand — run
> `go run ./tools/cvedoc` to regenerate after changing the catalog.

**1541 CVEs** in the catalog — 983 detectable (exploit-signatured), 558 advisory-only.

## Contents

- [Overview — top 10s](#overview--top-10s)
- [By protocol / service](#by-protocol--service)
- [By product / tool](#by-product--tool)
- [All CVEs](#all-cves)

### Highest EPSS (exploit probability)

EPSS is the [Exploit Prediction Scoring System](https://www.first.org/epss/) estimate (0–1) that a CVE will be exploited in the wild in the next 30 days — a
